data-go=\"115\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"117\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"x008c01","title":"1. A Swan at the Welcome","volume":"婚后外传：北角学院的失准目光","anchor":"chapter-0118","paragraphs":["The swan was not part of the presentation. It walked in through an open terrace door, pursued a pigeon under the chairs, and brought the eminent dean's welcome to an abrupt end. Mara held a screwdriver; Cassian carried tea. \"A little bit of audience feedback,\" he observed. \"Dazzlingly direct. Best welcome in history.\"","Northwestern Field College trained animal-care workers. Its donor exhibition was meant to culminate in a scholarship award. Mara had been hired to restore the old teaching display, which now included an eye-tracking system for a behavioral study of visitors. Her analytical attention settled on its loose mount. An image could degrade if the sensor moved; it was advisable to check the equipment before interpreting any result. The dean preferred photographs to lengthy tests. Students looked up to him.","Nina, a mature student, helped her detach the outer cover. She was the adoptive mother of Rosie, who was actively drawing an insect on the program. Nina's father was terminally ill. The scholarship would help her complete a long-term course while caring for him. Its graduation rate looked excellent in the prospectus. The list of students who had quietly left looked less impressive.","\"We hope to clean up the records,\" Nina said. \"Hopefully without humiliating anybody.\"","A monkey in the rehabilitation building grabbed a keeper's empty glove through a service hatch. Rosie laughed. Her mother asked her to keep an eye on the younger visitors while the adults worked. A frightened rabbit nearby was due to give birth to its first litter. Loud crowds could exacerbate its distress. The staff had centered the quiet route away from that room; the new exhibition map sent everybody past it instead.","Mara found the fault amid a dizzying tangle of cables. The repair was discernable once the case was open: a support had bent, leaving the sensor off-center. The dean would be bound to do himself a disservice if he used the damaged readings as proof of learning. A false conclusion was likely to do harm to students whose work would be judged by it. \"We must base our decision on the observations,\" she said. \"Not a beautiful screen.\"","He gave an authoritative smile and asked her to leave academic matters to him. Nina looked at the floor. Cassian, as always, became more courteous when somebody was being unreasonable. He asked which contractual term required the restorer to conceal a visible defect. The dean went momentarily dumb. He recovered by suggesting that an outsider's interference could polarize the staff.","Mara consciously set down her tool. She would not abdicate responsibility for what she signed. Visitors could be susceptible to suggestion, and a glowing label beside faulty results would not improve the study. Qualitative notes from the keepers had already recorded confusion. The dean could assert that the instrument was fine; assertion could not furnish the missing check.","A sponsor named Brant intervened. His foundation could alleviate the college's budget problem if the board accepted his preferred training contractor. He offered to shell out for a new system, but his private dinner invitation came with several restrictions on who could attend. Nina had received one, though other students with the same scores had not. The anti-bribery policy required gifts connected to procurement to be declared. Brant dismissed that as a formality.","\"Your influence could apply pressure on the board,\" Mara said.","\"My generosity could stimulate progress.\"","\"Both claims may be true. That is why the rule exists.\"","The exchange was brutal enough to stop the room's chatter. Cassian placed a cup in her hand before anyone could escalate it into a shouting match. He knew the fearsome look she wore when somebody assumed she would enjoy being purchased. \"Tea,\" he whispered. \"Before you turn the donor into an educational exhibit.\"","Outside, a keeper weighed food for a penguin. Its body fat was monitored as part of its care, not inferred from the way a visitor looked at a poster. Brant had used photographs of that work in his owned media to suggest he already funded the laboratory. At the time of the photograph, the actual equipment had been bought by the college. The same distinction was at issue in his new proposal: whose work would his logo claim?","Nina showed them the scholarship conditions. Salaried students with paid leave could attend the required sessions more easily than unpaid carers. The rule applied to both groups alike, but their respective burdens were different. The dean's plan counted only students who stayed until the last exam. Excluding those who had left made the graduation rate abundantly flattering. It did not prove the course was useless. It made the comparison advertised to donors incomplete.","Mara asked for the underlying lists. The dean said she lacked authority. She agreed; the trustees could request them. He had a clear choice about whether to cooperate voluntarily. He could not turn a dispute over that choice into evidence that Nina was incapable. A vicious remark about her \"inconvenient family\" made even Brant frown. Nina was overjoyed when a fellow student stepped beside her instead of moving away.","The trustees decided to inspect the records next morning. The dean called the delay an economic risk. Mara asked for the absolute cost of the proposed contractor's work, rather than a percentage without its base. He had brought no figure. The omission would not necessarily inflict a loss, but it was sufficient reason not to rush. An uncontrolled accusation could be inhuman; so could knowingly treating a person's care obligations as evidence of laziness.","They left abreast along the northwestern path. Cassian asked whether she regretted accepting a restoration job. \"Maybe a little.\" \"I should like to mobilize a quieter holiday.\" She took his arm. A healthy campus needed more than one dramatic intervention. People had to stay with the lengthy, ordinary work after the visitors went home. Ultimately, that work would decide whether the course improved.","An intermittent alarm interrupted them. Rosie moved headlong toward exposed wiring. Visitors must not come in contact with it. In times of confusion, Nina liked to immerse herself in work. Rosie pocketed her noise-making toy.","Behind them, Brant began to offer a new version of events. The dean would follow suit if it saved his position. The bargain carried a corruptive influence as well as financial risks: the promise of money could encourage a board to stop asking what it was buying. Mara glanced back. A good-natured keeper had put the swan outside, and the hall was quiet again. The Packet in the Rain","volume":"婚后外传：北角学院的失准目光","anchor":"chapter-0119","paragraphs":["Rain turned the college courtyard dazzlingly bright beneath its lamps. Mara and Cassian walked abreast under one umbrella, which covered him badly and her beautifully. \"Your sacrifice is appreciated,\" she said. \"My shoulder will be remembered in history.\" \"By a very small plaque.\" He kissed her temple and adjusted the umbrella. They had come to inspect the field records before the trustees met.","Nina waited amid the exhibits with a steamed bun and tea. Her terminally ill father sat nearby with a nurse, enjoying a brief visit to the garden. Nina's adoptive daughter had drawn him a fearsome lion. He was overjoyed by its enormous teeth. In times of worry, the child could furnish a room with creatures more readily than the college could furnish it with chairs.","The dean had accused Nina of copying a behavioral report. Her portfolio included seabird body fat estimates. A damaged eye-tracking record supposedly showed that she had seen confidential material. Her analytical notes were qualitative observations of visitors, not readings from that machine. A support remained visibly bent. To assert cheating on that basis was likely to do harm before any hearing could repair it. \"We should base an accusation on evidence,\" Mara said, \"not one centered on a convenient target.\"","Brant wanted them to proceed quickly. A contractual deadline, he said, threatened to culminate in an expensive delay. He would shell out for a new display if the board stopped indulging objections. The anti-bribery rule still required disclosure of the linked benefit. \"You can apply pressure on people without calling it pressure,\" Cassian said. \"That does not make it disappear.\" Brant had mistaken courteous speech for a promise not to interfere.","Mara had consciously brought the original repair photographs. The defect was discernable before Nina's visit. Results could degrade while an operator remained entirely honest. The dean tried to detach the dates from the question, claiming that machines had fluctuations anyway. An intermittent fault made the inference less reliable, not more authoritative. The explanation was abundantly clear to the technician whom he had not expected anyone to bring.","A missing page complicated the comparison. Nina had last seen it beneath the northwestern window. Brant said the absence proved nothing. On that point he was right; a missing page did not identify its remover. Mara chose to keep an eye on his hands while Cassian asked for the ordinary copy register. The donor reached for his briefcase at precisely the wrong moment.","He tried to contrive an escape with a packet marked PRIVATE. Cassian stepped into the doorway. Brant shoved past him, upsetting a jug. Water ran toward an open case of electrical equipment. Mara stopped the caretaker from charging headlong after the man. \"Isolate that first. It is advisable.\" Nobody should come in contact with a live wet connection. A training filter intended to purify tank water stood nearby. The caretaker dealt with the equipment while she went after the packet. An angry pursuit was bound to do damage if it forgot the people in its path.","Brant crossed the garden through a dizzying maze of temporary screens. Mara took the shorter route she had inspected while measuring the old display. At the gate he turned into a dead end, where a volunteer had parked a cart. He threw the packet over it. Cassian, who had gone around the other side, caught the papers against his chest. He gave a hearty laugh. \"A generous contribution,\" he said. \"I prefer donors who deliver.\"","The commission at issue would escalate the quarrel. The packet contained receipts and a copy of the very page they needed. The fees were not simply the absolute cost of equipment. They included a private commission tied to the contractor's selection. Such a bargain carried an economic risk and a corruptive influence together. The actual work might still be feasible, but the process used to choose it could not simply be declared clean because the final object looked attractive.","Brant called the challenge brutal. Nina quietly asked what word he used for threatening her scholarship. The eminent dean was struck dumb long enough for the trustee to take the packet. She would not abdicate the board's responsibility by treating a donor as untouchable. Adults and students alike could be susceptible to intimidation. The answer was a process that let them ask questions without sacrificing their respective futures to another person's vanity.","The dean's graduation rate excluded those who had left before the final assessment. The figure was not necessarily false under that narrow definition. It did not describe the chance of completing the whole course. Nina had been included in the marketing photograph, but threatened with disappearance from the denominator. The trustees could not alleviate that unfairness by offering a little bit of personal sympathy while leaving the calculation unchanged.","Mara asked Nina what she actually wanted. \"A fair assessment. Time to care for him. Not a special pass.\" Her father lifted his cup. \"And a less lengthy debate while my tea is warm.\" The nurse laughed. His illness was real, and nothing about a scholarship would cure it. A sensible timetable could make their remaining time less cruel. Its long-term value would exist even after the college stopped photographing it.","The rabbit's keeper passed with a quiet request to move the crowd. The animal might give birth to its litter that night. Making it perform for a donor would exacerbate distress. The keeper was actively trying to stimulate normal feeding, not a dramatic public response. \"Maybe we can all learn from her,\" Cassian murmured. Mara looked at her husband. \"I had planned to immerse you in a formal apology from the dean.\" \"An inhuman punishment.\"","The board would investigate the procurement and mobilize staff and bring in an independent assessor. It would not follow suit when the dean tried to polarize the dispute into loyalty or betrayal. The adviser found the linked gift arrangement sufficiently troubling to throw out the hurried approval request. That decision did not dispose of every technical problem. It supplied room to clean up the evidence without buying Brant's desired conclusion.","On the terrace, Cassian unfolded his wet coat and discovered the remains of their lunch. Mara offered him a biscuit. \"As always, I have provided for your survival.\" \"Your confidence will ultimately upend my diet.\" He ate it, then held one to her mouth. Hopefully, tomorrow would bring a fairer examination. Tonight they could agree that the most vicious part of the evening had not been Rosie's lion, and that the college had better things to rebuild than a donor's opinion of himself."],"questions":[],"wordCount":1099,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0119\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">婚后外传：北角学院的失准目光</div><h1>2. The eye-tracking result shifted before his eyes did.","The difference was small but discernable. It did not prove the whole behavioral project useless. It proved that an unreliable installation was likely to do harm if treated as authoritative evidence against a student. They must base their judgment on the observations. The assessor compared Nina's qualitative notes with the original dates. Their agreement was abundantly clear; the accusation was not.","A fair assessment was at issue. The dean could assert his confidence again, but he could not furnish a missing calibration record by sounding certain. Neither could he abdicate responsibility for the accusation he had endorsed. His brutal remark about Nina's father went into the complaint file. An adviser would review the case rather than let the same people who made it dictate its conclusion. The board would not escalate an accusation without evidence. A fair process was bound to do more good than another performance of institutional pride.","Mara helped detach the damaged support. She upended the empty case to show the loose fastening inside. It would not degrade a corrected record to admit what had been wrong. Visitors could come in contact with an accurate explanation instead of an unqualified promise. Cassian, struck dumb by the less technical spectacle of his wife balancing on a stool, offered a steadying hand. \"As always,\" she said, \"I appreciate assistance that does not become a speech.\"","Nina's assessment could now proceed. It centered on the skills she had actually studied. She explained the body fat estimates in her seabird-care portfolio, and why a single photograph would not establish a feeding plan. She helped clean up a specimen area, then demonstrated how a training filter could purify water. The exam was practical, a little bit dull in places, and much more demanding than smiling beneath a sponsor's sign.","An intermittent alarm distracted the group. Nina did not rush headlong into the service room. She asked the technician to check whether the signal indicated a fault or a test. It was a planned check. Panic could exacerbate a problem; indifference could conceal one. Her response was proportionate, and the examiner wrote it down. She had kept an eye on the living things in her care without pretending that every sound was a disaster.","The rabbit did give birth to four young that morning. Noise-making exhibits were kept outside. Only the keepers saw the event, leaving the mother space. Rosie drew a small tail on each baby and asked hopefully when they would open their eyes. Nina promised to ask the keeper, rather than invent an answer for an eager child. She knew enough to realise the difference between comforting confidence and knowledge.","Her terminally ill father watched from the quiet terrace. A chair, shelter, and a nurse had alleviated the strain of the visit without changing his condition. Nina's adoptive daughter sat beside him with her notebook. Students and families alike had helped bring him out for the morning. Their respective contributions were modest; together they made a difficult day kinder. In times of fear, kindness could still be actively organized.","The board would mobilize funds for the independent appeal and correct the graduation rate in future publicity. It would include clear information about those who enrolled and those who completed. The statistics would not become more flattering. They would become more useful. Nina passed her observed tasks; that result would culminate in a properly recorded qualification after the remaining checks, not a special diploma created to soothe embarrassment.","The anti-bribery review would continue separately. Brant's linked offer had carried a corruptive influence regardless of how attractive the machine looked. Donors could shell out without buying authority over an assessment. Nobody would follow suit merely because his competitors had accepted the same arrangement. A donor could apply pressure on a board; a board could consciously refuse. The adviser would not inflict an unproven accusation on every person who had attended the dinner.","The trustees chose not to throw out the entire curriculum. The old fees were dizzying. Revised contractual terms and a new timetable looked feasible, with options for carers as well as salaried students. School visits could bring in a modest income to support it. At the time of the next grant review, the college would report the actual absolute cost and the remaining economic risk. Its owned media would publish the correction. If the mass media preferred only the scandal, the quieter record would still exist.","Mara watched Nina hug the assessor and then her father. The student was overjoyed. Her classmates had not all become friends, but the dean's effort to polarize them had failed. A lengthy dispute could have left a vicious division. Instead, several asked how to keep the revised system honest. Long-term trust needed sustained work, not one dazzlingly successful morning. No one photograph could stand for it.","The father wanted a hearty lunch. Cassian found steamed fish from the kitchen and contrived a table with room for the wheelchair. \"You may be the most useful gentleman in history,\" Mara told him. \"A terrifying title.\" \"Fearsome, even.\" She kissed him before he could attempt a ceremonial bow. The nurse laughed, and Nina's father said the entertainment had improved since the speeches stopped.","They walked abreast to the northwestern terrace and ate. Mara immersed herself in the simple pleasure of a meal with no accusation attached. They would comply with the remaining checks. \"Amazing,\" Cassian murmured. It was advisable to enjoy the peace before somebody offered another astonishing restoration commission. \"Maybe we should stimulate the business with less exciting clients,\" Cassian suggested. \"Furniture, perhaps.\" \"Furniture can be susceptible to expensive claims.\" \"I knew you would find a danger.\"","Nina said her daughter would look up to them both. Mara hoped she would also ask them questions. It would be inhuman to expect a child never to be mistaken, and dumb to teach her that important adults never were. Ultimately, the lesson was not distrust of everything. It was the courage to check what mattered. Cassian took Mara's hand beneath the table. A Cabbage and a Photograph","volume":"婚后外传：河岸集市失窃案","anchor":"chapter-0121","paragraphs":["At the riverside fair, Cassian won the booby prize before Mara had finished her first coffee. His paper airplane had travelled backward into the mayor's hat. The prize was a cabbage. He offered it to his wife with the gravity of an embassy appointment. \"A rare bloom,\" he said. \"Locally grown.\"","She kissed him beside the bookstore and took the cabbage. An old billboard advertised the aeroplane display beyond the dam; below it, a blackboard promised guitar music in the factory courtyard. This former industrial village was trying to broaden its income without turning every dwelling into a souvenir shop. Visitors could camp near the barn or rent a cozy cottage. The aggregate proceeds would repair the public footpath. Ada, the young archivist who had invited Mara, had paid their baggage charge herself.","Ada had a more urgent problem than the cabbage. A daguerreotype lent for the fair had disappeared from a locked case. A modern photographer had made it in 1992, using the old process for a historical-photography exhibition. It showed her grandmother beside a replica of an early aircraft, with a child whose name had been scratched from the mount. A woman who had worked at Harvard University had recently offered to examine it. The picture might clarify parent-child kinship in Ada's family; it was also a fragile original, not a commodity that could simply be replaced.","\"I view the photograph as a family obligation,\" Ada said. \"Lyle views it as publicity.\"","Lyle, the producer, arrived in an auto too polished for the gravel. He wore an overly nostalgic flying jacket and carried an unlit cigar. His quasi-official badge made him look like a policeman, although headquarters had given him no such authority. He proposed a shocking shortcut: announce the plate destroyed, claim insurance, and put up a fake. \"Duplication is economical,\" he said. \"The public cannot tell.\"","\"Then the decision should rest with Ada,\" Cassian replied.","Ada would stipulate written permission before any new copy was made. A sane lender protected an original. Their tour began beneath an engraving of the local cathedral. The adjacent chapel stored costumes; the old gaol held a historical exhibition. A cigaret advertisement hung beside leaflets from a former councillor on the ultra-right. A constituent had once challenged him about housing. The graphic account of that argument, preserved on cassette, was more interesting than his elevated claims about public duty. Ada switched off the recording when Lyle interrupted her again.","Mara found a smear of cleanser beneath the empty case. A clamp that secured its back had been moved, and a wooden baluster beside the stair had a new scrape. Upstairs, an elevator served the dorm used by visiting performers. Its door opened directly onto a hoist above the loading yard. Someone could move a flat parcel without passing the main entrance. Cassian tested the loose rail before allowing Mara to lean over it, then lifted her lightly back to the floor. \"Your ankles are an asset I am personally invested in.\"","Below them, a keeper tried to coax a capuchin away from a stolen ticket roll. The monkey had escaped its travel carrier during a supervised educational visit. Its safe recovery made the yard briefly abuzz. Mara noticed Lyle watching the hoist rather than the animal. Beside him stood a hamper marked for the cocktail lounge, which occupied a converted railway carriage. Mara could see the fore end of a canoe protruding from a tarpaulin at the carriage’s river landing.","An actor crossed the yard with a toy gun. Its painted bullet was tied to a string, and his prop bomb was nothing more threatening than a striped stage box. An electrician replaced a fuse in the show lights while a carpenter carried an axe back to the workshop. Lyle complained that these visible repairs would hamper his publicity. \"A classic attraction needs confidence,\" he insisted.","\"Confidence doesn't make a chimney safe,\" Ada said. The stove below it was blocked and might choke the room with smoke. The carpenter was already at work. He thought the risks were controllable if the closed area stayed closed. A drill lay ready for a small bracket repair; no one would light the stove before the sweep returned. Lyle said the inspection was time-consuming. Ada said she would stick with the closure. It was the first occasion that morning on which she had sounded happy with herself.","At lunch Cassian opened the hamper, expecting sandwiches. Under a disposable plate lay a brass compass and a tiny sealed capsule. The capsule held a rolled catalogue label, not medicine. The label described a reproduction sold for one buck at a long-closed fair. Rain had blurred the ink, but the date remained clear. Lyle dismissed it as worthless; Cassian suggested a dose of common sense before they threw away a dated record. It preceded the supposed destruction of the original photograph by thirty years.","Lyle tried to laugh. \"A harmless relic.\"","Mara touched his sleeve. \"Then why is it in baggage booked onto tonight's helicopter?\"","His expression changed. The highway was shut for a bridge inspection; the helicopter was the only quick departure. He called her suspicious, then helpless, then unreasonable. Cassian put down the compass with a quiet click. \"Choose a different adjective.\"","Ada led them to her cottage office. A dryer hummed behind the kitchen, rescuing damp visitors' coats. On the table, a classic guide to metalwork showed how a silver plate could be copied, but not perfectly. Mara could spot a modern tool mark; she could not responsibly declare an old image authentic by touch. They needed the mounting record. Ada remembered storing it beneath a flagstone beside the old forge, where her grandmother had hidden letters during a flood.","Outside, a local policymaker greeted an influential donor. He wanted an upfront promise that no public money had vanished. Ada offered access to the accounts, but refused to put a quart into a pint pot by staging the concert, running the archive and conducting an inquiry alone. Cassian volunteered to carry chairs. Mara volunteered to find the missing mount.","\"Terrific,\" said Ada. \"But don't lose control of the monkey.\"","The keeper waved from the gate. The capuchin was safely in its carrier. Lyle, however, was no longer in the yard.
